# Active arcs

One line per arc, newest first within each group. Each entry names its own
page; the page is the record, this is the way in.

- `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-10-05b.md` (NUDE-HOSTS, DECOR-MODS, DECOR-DUNGEON, DECOR-OUTDOOR, DECOR-LPT, AUDIT 05b) - 2026-10-05, the owner: "There seems to be a lot of missing decor items with house decoration, plus elements should recieve the low poly overhaul style like trees got" and "Even with nudity turned off. Players can see and have access to nude vendors": the hosts made after NUDE-FLATS never asked its table - the decorator offered, stood, flew and pictured the nude figures among its Vendors, and the Arena's tiers seated two - now gated by Show Nudity as the world's people are, every billboard host of src/ named by a sweep so the next one cannot forget; and the decorator's catalogue offers the town mods' furnishings while they stand, 297 pieces measured over the mods' own blocks, after every name of Daggerfall's (DECOR-MODS), reads the dungeons' furnishings (DECOR-DUNGEON), no room's name moving, and a yard's the streets' pieces and its climate's trees and plants, drawn in its town's climate and season (DECOR-OUTDOOR); a placed tree or plant Low Poly Trees has a tree for stands as the world's do - its 3D tree near the eye, its far picture beyond, the decorator's ghost the same (DECOR-LPT). AUDIT 05b ("audit this"): thirteen findings fixed and pinned - a tree landing a recentre off its yard, the mods' pieces fixed once a session (now asked as offered), a yard rebuilt under the open decorator, the ghost's climate and a leak, and one choice of a nature flat's picture for every host (`world/naturePicture.js`).

It is Daggerfall's own textures repainted, so VE3 had the player attach their own .dfmod files; Mac approved carrying it ("Put it in the codebase"), and VE4 ships the Base, Masked Roads and Snowless Swamps and Jungles under Port-Doctrine's one exception - 1,436 pictures from the mod's repository at a pinned commit, its terrain arrays served from the pictures they were proved within BC7's error of, registered in the texture-mod door beside the attached - the Base on by default (AUDIT VE: "Ensure this is on by default"), its add-ons off, the decode a worker's. The texture-mod door gets DFU's load order (AutoSortMods' dependencies first, TryGetAsset's last-loaded answering on every door - the add-ons had lost every name to their Base) and DFU's terrain import whole (TryImportTextureArray and GetTerrainTextureArray: a loose record 0 first, the first mod's array or records deciding, an array at the archive's depth exactly, a records-built set at record 0's size); mods switch on and off (Mod.Enabled); the Texture Overhaul card holds Classic and Vanilla Enhanced, its add-ons switched on the card. Not done: a mod's Materials (Ledger C), and a probe over a player's real 3.5.0 bundles.

'Ruins of Old Carololda's Farm'"; Aru: "enemies can attack you while the result loads"; Cruor: "New fishing context pop up clashes with come sail away!"), a relayed line ("audio cutting when taking helm of a ship") and Mac's "Enhanced climbing needs a further perfection audit". The climb run on 25 real dungeons: a 3.2 m unit's jamb over the climber is passed by moving along the wall, the press is to the face and not through it (the push out of a deep one slid the climb down a diagonal seam), a 2.5 cm crack between a wall's pieces is no lip, a face set back over the hands is climbed on to, and in a corner the top the look is turned to is taken. Every wall the classic climb topped is topped, except slopes past 50 degrees and a lip with no landing right behind it. A foe come near closes the hunt's ask and search, and the search's minutes pass at the close. No fishing cast at the helm, over laid guns or in a boarding. A boat's playing loop is not restarted by a crossfade, and a stopped fade or crossfade lands where it was going. Then audited (Mac: "Audit this"; the record's "The audit"): the corner's side read once a hold, a failed sidestep search kept, a save in the step's pass retaking at reach, the step passed across too, climbing down reaching as the grab does, the hold turning only to a face met along its normal, the crack measured; a load closes the hunt's box first, a box taken away spends its minutes quiet, a duel's foe is near. `test/fb1002_climb.test.js`, `test/fb1002_fieldbugs.test.js`, `test/fb1002_audit.test.js`.

- `03-World/Parkour-Arc.md` CLIMB1 and AUDIT CLIMB1 - 2026-09-30, Mac ("I want to talk about building enhanced climbing... latch, mantle, jump from one location to another ledge. Almost parkour like"; the calls "Jump is the grab", "Skill scales it", "Free-climb on grip", "Parkour leap, skill-scaled"; then "let's do an audit on this. I want to ensure it's perfect and 1:1 with what I want"): THE LEDGE, THE MANTLE, THE CLAMBER AND THE VAULT - a lip within the hands' reach (1.5 m at Climbing 0 to 2.1 m at 100, +0.15 in the air) is climbed onto, or over a thin top, along a scripted path proven clear at every point before it starts: Jump pressed at it on the ground, Jump held as a jump or a fall carries a lip into reach (held only for a fall the skill can hold, 5-15 m; since CLIMB2 a lip at the chest or higher is held, unless Forward is), or the climb arriving under it (the top-out, no key - since CLIMB2 the free climb's); a waist-high thin top is vaulted moving forward, never into a fall that hurts; a crouch's room ends crouched. A jump's fatigue each, a mantle trains Climbing and a vault Jumping; Roleplay & Realism's no climbing with a weapon out holds. The audit: four lenses, 19 findings and 5 mutation survivors, every one pinned red and fixed - the worst a crouched mantle that ended standing on the ceiling and a path proven only at its ends (through railings and lintels). The enhanced lane's, `enhancedClimbing` on by default; offline the classic skin keeps DFU's climb, online it is on for everyone whatever their skin; `?parkour=off`. `test/parkour.test.js` (17), `test/auditclimb1.test.js` (20); `tools/mutants/climb1.json` (21) and `auditclimb1.json` (35), all dead. Not seen on real ARENA2 geometry. CLIMB2 SHIPPED the same day (Mac: "Yes"): THE HANG, THE SHIMMY, THE GRIP AND THE FREE CLIMB - a lip caught in the air at the chest or higher is held (Forward or a fresh Jump climbs up, Back climbs down, Crouch lets go), Left and Right shimmy along it and round a building's corners, inner and outer, stopping where it ends; on the enhanced lane any wall is free-climbed in place of the classic climb and its rolls - Forward held against it (0.6-0.3 s), or Forward and Jump held in the air - up, down and across at the skill's pace, its top held and climbed over; the GRIP (6 s at Climbing 0 to 30 s at 100, a third of it at no Fatigue) is spent on the wall and returns on the ground, a meter on both HUDs; a full pack cuts the reach 0.3 m; the wall bills the climbing Fatigue band (the interior ticker's missing arm fixed). `test/climb2.test.js` (25), `tools/mutants/climb2.json` (59), all dead. AUDIT CLIMB2 (2026-10-01, Mac: "Hey audit this and ensure its perfection before we merge"): four lenses, 24 findings and the mutation run's own, each pinned red and fixed - a save on the wall keeps the hold, roofs' eaves are holds, the shimmy goes round towers and rooms of any shape, the free climb never steps into what it climbs, leans out past rails and reaches round cornices, a corner is the same hold going on, the grip spent in every move and back treading water; `test/auditclimb2.test.js` (24), `tools/mutants/auditclimb2.json` (56). Then what was left (Mac: "Take care of the what is left including a jump catching a ledge"): a tapped Jump catches a high ledge, the fit is fine enough that a thin feature sits no deeper than a contact, and `test/climbreal.test.js` climbs Daggerfall's own buildings wherever the game data is - still unrun, no session having had it. With main #498 merged, its climbing past 100 is carried onto this lane: a live Climbing past 100 climbs and shimmies faster here too (online the classic climb never runs). PR #483 merged. Then the rest of the arc (2026-10-01, Mac: "So plsyers can get stuck on the very top of roofs", "I reallty want go to go all in with the detai. Liike proer feel to climbing", "Take care of the remaining items"): CLIMB-DOWN (the way down from a roof - over a parapet into a hang, crouched off an edge into a hang; slow climbs leave the terrain), CLIMB3 (the leaps - side, up and the eject from a hold, the running leap and its late press, the wall run, on past a sill), CLIMB4 (the feel - the camera, eleven synthesised sounds and the effort's voice, the weapon lowered off the hands on the wall), CLIMB5 (the climb on the wire, RELAY_VERSION world141 - world138 on the branch, renumbered at the merge of main - not yet deployed - the others see a climber facing the wall, off the ground, and hear the climb; the own body in third person faces the wall) and PERF-CLIMB (a capsule resolve that moved nothing stops - to the bit, a quarter to two thirds off every resolve). Pins `test/climbdown.test.js` (15), `test/climb3.test.js` (17), `test/climb4.test.js` (19), `test/climb5.test.js` (7), `test/climbperf.test.js` (3); mutants `tools/mutants/climbdown.json` (40), `tools/mutants/climb3.json` (59), `tools/mutants/climb4.json` (75), `tools/mutants/climb5.json` (50), `tools/mutants/climbperf.json` (3) - 225 dead, 2 recorded equivalent, every survivor of the first pass pinned. AUDIT CLIMB-ARC (2026-10-01, Mac: "a detailed audit on this to make sure it's perfect"): four lenses over PR #504, every finding reproduced on d92a75a13 and pinned red, then fixed - the feel (the corner's turn mirrored, a held frame replaying the climb, 60 Hz travel read at 144 Hz, Euler springs, the leap's dip at its push-off, the dungeons aiming with the felt view), the leaps (stairs as an edge, a leap shorter than the jump, the late press steered by the view and fired off a step, Slowfall and wading, a standstill wall run, moves unbilled), the way down (Forward held climbing back up, the drawn ground, the parapet trap), the others (the sprite lane's yaw, the beast's stride, the classic climb's facing, the body chasing a turning view, the switch replaying, no sound floor); `test/auditclimbarc.test.js` (34), `tools/mutants/auditclimbarc.json` (71) - 70 dead, 1 recorded equivalent. Not yet looked at on a real install. Four findings, each pinned red first: the fixed-city host (exterior.js) sent its street's watch running and left a building's standing (F1); a player asked as a beast who had changed back could still roar, wiping a man's crime (F2); a routed watchman read as a pacified one, so a blow on him turned every pacified creature around hostile and friendly protection spared him from swings (F3); and, met in the merge of #464, REP1's stop could walk a beast that changed under its box into court by "come quietly" (F4). - `06-Systems/Systems-Arc.md` (WERE-FRIGHT) - 2026-09-29, Mac ("Can you make it where being a werewolf has a different interaction with guards? Where you cannot surrender, but instead a chance to frighten?"; asked: "Flee, crime dropped", "Your level vs theirs", "Werewolves and wereboars"): THE BEAST CANNOT SURRENDER; IT ROARS. A transformed werewolf or wereboar halted by the watch is offered F - frighten and N - fight on in place of the surrender question (`arrestFlow.js beastHaltBox`), and a fatal blow no longer forces a surrender on a beast. The roar rolls 50%, five points a level either way against the striking guard's level, held to 10-90 - which is 20-35% against the real watch, minted three to six levels above the player. Frightened, the crime is forgotten and every watchman of it runs for five seconds and walks away (`cityGuards.frighten`, `EnemyAI.flee` - the pursuit's own turn, walk, detour and knockback, `_walkStep` and `_knockbackStep` extracted from `_step` so both run one copy), the defenders holding and the witnesses' watch called off; unafraid, the blow lands. Not driven online. 12 pins in `test/werefright.test.js`; `tools/mutants/werefright.json`, 29, all dead.
- `04-Characters/Steel-Brigandine.md` (MW-BRIG3) - 2026-09-29, Mac ("the session that was working on it completely broke the morrowind torso"; asked: worn only, a male body, "it's placed lower where the torso should be"): THE BRIGANDINE FITTED ONTO THE WEARER. MW-BRIG1 and MW-BRIG2 both drew it at the height of the modeller's scene, taken on faith as the skeleton at rest and never measured on a body; that scene's body stood lower than the Morrowind body (the brigandine's collar peaks at z 94.8, its belt at ~67), so it sat under the torso and, worn as a cuirass that hides the chest skin, left the chest and shoulders bare. MW-BRIG2's stated cause (the Chest node drifting in the idle) was not what was seen. Now `fitTo: 'chest'` moves it onto the wearer at bind time - its top to the chest's top in the rest pose, measured skinned (`fitLift`) - before it is skinned from the body; a pure lift, the shape and texture untouched, a build note naming the lift. 6 pins in `test/mwbrig3.test.js`, the bug first, on the shipped mesh.
- `06-Systems/Standing-Arc.md` (REP1-REP6 THE REPUTATION OVERHAUL) - 2026-09-29, Mac: "So I want to talk about overhauling the reputation system. Something just much better and not as punishing, but still punishing"; his calls "Challenged on sight", "The charge, with a mark", "Timed or pardoned", "Earn it + faster drift". BUILT: the 5%-a-minute Conspiracy levy is retired - a guard who sees a known criminal stops them (pay the court's Conspiracy fine in coin, come quietly, refuse), two game hours between stops, a day's grace after the law is answered; a sentence gives the charge back (an Assault, a Murder, a Treason keep half), one charge per chase, a beaten criminal is taken alive unless a watchman fell; banishment only for a Murder or a Treason, 30 days of the world's calendar or a temple's pardon; a bad name recovers a point a week, a temple's penance buys five, a bounty contract two; the law of every region on the Standing page with its price and a notice on every change; guild probation before expulsion. Port-Ledger A. Pinned by `test/rep1_watchstop.test.js` - `test/rep6_probation.test.js` (32) and fifteen older files moved with the law; `tools/mutants/rep*.json` (64, all dead). Not verified in a browser.

- `06-Systems/Travel-View.md` OW-WOD - 2026-09-29, Mac ("When near mountains from WOD, the game lags insane"; "Pathing doesnt go around mountains"; "Pathing doesnt follow the road around cities"): three root causes. THE LAG was the collider's coarse grid - a query near a World of Daggerfall massif took every face over its column and tested each exactly - and is a bounding-volume tree over each bucket's wide faces now (1.5-5.7 ms to 0.1-0.37 ms a body's move on a stand-in, exact against brute force), built inside the pixel's build; the road-clearance walk asks only the map's pixels. THE ROUTE knew only the MAPS climate's peaks, and refuses an open step into a WOD massif's pixel now (2,492 pixels, less those a path crosses). THE TOWNS: a route through a location's pixel walks its border ring, Travel Options' own, in on the incoming side and out on the outgoing - never a leg at the town's heart. Left open: no climb price on open ground (the heightmaps are the player's). `test/ow_wod.test.js`, `tools/mutants/owwod.json`.

RAIN-SPRINKLE: rain 0.05..1 rolled toward the light end, a system's edge drizzling and its heart pouring, and a light fall's fog, sun and grass an overcast sky's (`fallTerms`). OW-WEATHER: what falls wraps round the Overworld's eye, not the head under it. OW-FACE: the body faces the keys' way. OW-PEERS: the others grown as the traveller is. COAST-RAY: a coast's breakers rebuilt in milliseconds (a straight-down ray asks its column once). - `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-10-01.md` (MARKET-KEEP, MARKET-ANY, ROAD-LOT, YARD-CORNER, YARD-STALE, YARD-RECENTRE, LOOK-BUTTONS, LOOK-STALE, LOOK-TRIED, RENT-REST, RENT-RENEW, RENT-FRESH, RENT-ORPHANS, RENT-NUMBER, HOMES-FORCE, MOVE-BANK, CLIMB-PAST) - 2026-10-01, a list of seven: the yard, the market, the paint, the rent, the road, the potions, the climb past 100. ROAD-LOT: a yard's lot is no road - the pixel's path tiles and the road painter's mask, refused and cut out of the marked edge. YARD-CORNER: a turned piece's ground, not its corners, against the house. YARD-STALE: a town read from before a write stands nothing back. YARD-RECENTRE: yards move in place the frame the world recentres. LOOK-BUTTONS: the painter's Paint it / Put back / The town's own stood in the rent tab's row, which the decorator's sheet hides - a look could only be tried. LOOK-STALE and HOMES-FORCE: one root, one rule - a forced town read is asked after the flight it finds (unless that flight was forced after the last write), and an answer from before a write is not believed. RENT-REST: a tenant rests in the room they rent. RENT-RENEW: the renewal window offers only the days the service takes. RENT-FRESH, RENT-ORPHANS, RENT-NUMBER: the owner's tab is read again, lists a one-room house's offers, and numbers a room as its door does. MANA-HALF and MANA-SHOP (the potions as the mage's repair bill) shipped in #498 and were withdrawn the same day: online rest and Restore Power are as before. MARKET-KEEP: a crafted piece the service calls another's (a trade never moves its owner) goes back to the pack - List destroyed it. MARKET-ANY: a realm character's pack piece lists for gold alone, taken out of the seller's record in the listing's batch and put into the buyer's at the collect; migration 0044, acct44, deploys with the merge. MOVE-BANK: past 100 a mastered skill has no 20,000-use bucket and a rest's leftover is carried whole - a run is counted however long. CLIMB-PAST: past 100 each effective Climbing point climbs 1% faster (Daggerfall's one Climbing law is certain at 95). `test/fb1001_*.test.js`, `tools/mutants/fb1001_*.json`.
- `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-30b.md` part two (FISH-TIRED, SWIM-SPENT, TOOL-SAID then TOOL-USE, BANK-REGION, FALL-HOLD, RES-RING, RES-MARK, HOME-PLAQUE, TV-SURF) - 2026-09-30, five screenshots and Mac's "Also you can get instakilled when fishing"; on the first answer, "I dont care about DFU. SWIM-SPENT: a swimmer who runs out of fatigue loses a tenth of the health every five seconds in the water, told to get out - DFU's instant death gone. TOOL-USE: online a profession tool's Use from the hotbar at a node of its own kind is E there, and anywhere else it gives none of Foraging's yields and says where the profession is done. BANK-REGION: the curse took nothing; the Enhanced Plus teller names its region's account and lists the others. FALL-HOLD: a foe over a pixel not built is held, not stepped - a camp's foe a load re-minted there fell for ever, each step dearer. RES-RING, RES-MARK, HOME-PLAQUE: a marked quest residence is rung on the Enhanced town sheet, one who knows it always marks it, and a nameless house with verbs is a "Residence" (its first click was HOME-OFFER's). TV-SURF: Come Sail Away's breakers are not drawn under the travel view. `test/fb0930b_*.test.js` (31), `tools/mutants/fb0930b_*.json` (95, 95 dead).

- `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-29h.md` (BOOT-HIDE, TOUCH-HELD, TEMPLE-SHELF, SPAWN-PLATE, TOKEN-WAIT, BOUNTY-LAIR, LOST-BOAT, INFECTION-KEPT, WALK-CLOCK, DEED-PORT) - 2026-09-29, eleven #bug-reports threads through Mac. BOOT-HIDE (Joctaed's "Cannot access 'be' before initialization", read off a rebuild whose chunk is the report's byte for byte): the realm's page-hide hook, the exit autosave, the saved-soon door and the title exit were handed to the page ahead of `bootWorld`'s awaits and read `online` in its dead zone - registered below the duel's now, a gate walking every page door. TOUCH-HELD ("(Mobile) Can't accept terms of entry for palaces"): the world host's `overlayActive` read the town's slot alone, so the nav row's abc and Return never stood over a castle's terms box - it reads the modes' stacks. BOUNTY-LAIR (targets in unreachable rooms): the lair stands at a quest spawn marker. LOST-BOAT (two Large Boats singing under Kinging Court): a boat the port lost before FIELD-CSA1 is given back as its parts. Then Mac, on the three first called Daggerfall's own: "Dont worry abour DFU." - three departures. INFECTION-KEPT (a plague's cure ended a lycanthropy on its way): a cure takes the plain diseases first, an infection when it is all that is left. WALK-CLOCK (32 days travelled, no rank): online an accelerated journey's minutes past the world's are the character's. DEED-PORT (no port in port towns): IsNearPort's square centred, the map's harbours counted, the refusal names the nearest. Said, not changed: the lighting (28f's DISC29-E) - four For Mac.
- `10-UI/Features-Arc.md` (TEXT-PASS) - 2026-09-29, Mac's text for the Overhaul menu ("only the description has been tweaked"; "UI colour", "Left stick run"; "I would use a slash between every button use that has more than one function"), and the ask with it: "audit text in general for descriptions and menus, reduce bloat and overall make it less AI". The Overhaul panel's five blurbs are Mac's words verbatim (one typo, "Daggefall", mended); the Enhanced Plus card's two labels are his; the controller window's two dotted labels and the d-pad legend take a slash. Every Features note rewritten in plain words - the engine terms (navmesh, tonemap, SKY*.DAT, mipmap), the version numbers, the "1:1" openers and the quoted mod taglines out; what the switch does, what Off does, what online changes and what a row needs kept - 16,634 characters down to 13,293, and FT15's ceiling falls to match; the DFU rows end "On/Off by default in Daggerfall Unity." The Online pane's footer (~1,900 characters) and its seven lock notes said plainly, the social toggles and the HUD scale row shortened. The mods' own per-setting descriptions are untouched (they are the mods' words, carried 1:1), and so is every classic Daggerfall string. Every pin that read the old wording now reads the new, each still holding the fact it held.

- `04-Characters/Steel-Brigandine.md` (MW-BRIG2) - 2026-09-29, Mac ("The texture was great, you just somehow moved the geometry in the process"): MW-BRIG1 moved the torso in game and was reverted (#451). The cause: it hung the brigandine rigid on the skeleton's Chest and Groin nodes with their rest pose taken out, while the body it covers is SKINNED to the spine and pelvis by its own NiSkinData (Morrowind-Rules.md rule 20) - two transforms, agreeing at rest and nowhere else, so once the idle played the brigandine and the torso came apart. Now the brigandine is SKINNED FROM THE BODY UNDER IT (`formats/mwSkinTransfer.js`): each vertex copies the skin of the nearest body vertex (the player's own chest, groin, thigh and knee parts, loaded even while the cuirass hides the chest) and is drawn by skinBatch, the body's own door - one piece, the skirt bending with the legs, no split, no rest-pose attach. The rigid path is untouched. 5 pins in `test/mwbrig2.test.js`, the bug itself first.
- `04-Characters/Steel-Brigandine.md` (MW-BRIG1) - 2026-09-29, Mac ("This is for the morrowind model. The steel brigantine"): THE PORT'S OWN WORN MODEL. Asked, Mac said it was FITTED IN PLACE on the Morrowind body, so the bake keeps the scene placement (`fbxMesh.mjs --placement=scene`: the object's whole T*R*S, the file's own axes and units) and the binder takes each bone's rest transform back out from the player's own skeleton (`restPoseInverse`) - at rest exactly where it was fitted, then riding the bone. The FIELD-GUN-MW1 bake refused concave faces; the brigandine has six, and Blender draws them ear-clipped, so the bake ear-clips them now (convex faces still fan - the Thunderlock re-bakes to the same bytes). `characters/ownArmorModels.js` is the worn counterpart of the Thunderlock's table; `composeWornArmor` claims its parts as it claims a record's. Every other material keeps its retail cuirass. Not yet looked at in game on a real body; the sources' licence is Mac's to confirm. Its attach moved the torso and was reverted (#451); MW-BRIG2 above replaced it, and its pins (mwbrig1, DELETED) with `test/mwbrig2.test.js`; 3 more in `test/fbxmesh.test.js`.

- `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-29e.md` (FIELD-WIND1) - 2026-09-29, the Discord through Mac: "A repetitive moaning sound in the open world". Found live on the retail data first: outdoors at Daggerfall AmbientWindMoan started 45 times in two minutes, back to back. The port's wind (WIND3) played DAGGER.SND's wind clips - DFU's dungeon one-shots, short, shaped and pitched - on the riding loop's shape, a clip started again each time it ends, so the moan (1.96 s that swells and falls with a voice in it) came back every 1.97 s under every breeze. The wind is a bed the port makes now (`windAudio.js` `windBedSamples`: seeded white noise under a band-pass and a low-pass, periodic so the native loop has no seam, at the moan's own RMS) on one native loop (`audio.registerSamples`, `loop`'s new `setPitch`); WIND3's gain law untouched. Seen live after: no moan started in 90 s, one looping source for the whole run.

BUILT: TWO CLOCKS - the world's (WORLD5, nobody's to move: the sky, the calendar, everything shared) and each character's OWN (worldTick.js ownMinutes: the world's offline, one variable; online it runs with the world's while they play, ahead of it by every RaiseTime - rest, loiter, journey, training, sentence, the turn, meals, the collapse, the cures - and stands while they are away or dead). The body, its magic, its needs, its contracts (rooms, loans, repairs, letters) and its standing (skill checks, training, guild rank waits, the drift, the racial quests) read it; the sun, the moons, the calendar, prices, spawns' night and day and quest clocks (WORLD7, OPEN 1) read the world's. An infection now counts the days the character lives, rests and journeys included. Retires RESTX2's session counter, MAC-LVL1's credit, REST-ROUNDS/RISE-REST F2's online arm, WORLD5 C1/C3/C6/C9/C14, the revival's shifts and OL2/OL3's online words; fixes the double disease roll, the free needs reset, the room that said expired and stayed. - `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-29c.md` (FIELD-CSA2) - 2026-09-29, the Discord's #general through Mac: ItMustBeMonday "I can't get my boat to work", SylviaBun "people are saying Ports are bugged for player boats" - still so hours after the night's fixes were live. Reproduced live on the retail data first: a deed's Small Ship on the Bay south of Daggerfall read its five nodes as land and the oars moved it 0 m. With Iliac Puddle No More on (the default) Come Sail Away's node law is `SampleHeight(node) < 34`, the sea's own height; Unity's 16-bit heightmap holds the flat sea at 33.994 m, the port's stand-in read the drawn ground's 34.000001 - so no boat on the sea ever rowed, raised a sail ("Unable to raise sail. Boat is obstructed.") or launched on an Overworld crossing, and every deed's boat sat by its port. Terrain.SampleHeight at Unity's precision now (`terrainSurface.js` `terrainSampleHeightAt`, read by `world.js` `csaTerrainOf`); no departure. It was Mac's 28e "at a port but unable to set sail" too - SHIP-PORT's "Not changed" corrected.

The mint signs whether the character it names is one of the account's realm characters (`rc`, acct22) and the relay refuses one that is not, in every room, with words that build prints (world130). - `06-Systems/Quest-Guide-Arc.md` (GUIDE1-GUIDE5) - 2026-09-29, Mac: "How can we set the foundation and improve the quest system substantially? Like really modernize it, make it more accessible". THE QUEST GUIDE: a layer of the port's own over the 1:1 quest machine, never in it. GUIDE1 SHIPPED, THE QUEST LENS (`src/ui/questLens.js`, one per quest bridge, reset on a load): every modern quest face's one read-only picture - each quest's entries in the order they were written, the latest, the clock, the target (DFU's GetLastPlaceMentionedInMessage, now one home with the classic logbook) and of it only the names the entry says - and what changed between two looks (started, updated, urgent, completed, ended; a load a baseline). Invisible to the machine: the quiet read keeps the reveal, the three latches, the DFRandom seed and the quest's rolls where they were - pinned over all 3,817 corpus messages and a whole game played twice. The one visible change: the enhanced journal's "latest" entry is the one written last (a step logged again showed a superseded entry). `test/guide1_questLens.test.js` (7), `tools/mutants/guide1.json` 38 (37 dead, one equivalent as recorded). Then Mac: "This is your baby. Take your time" - the arc is its own to finish, its open questions decided on its page. GUIDE2 SHIPPED, THE WAY THERE: the enhanced journal says where a quest points (the latest entry's place in DFU's find-place words, only the names it says) and takes you there - DFU's own logbook click, which the default skin never had: the pause tab and the chronicle close and the street's map door opens on the place, or refuses in DFU's words; buildings, dungeons and the fixed-town route say where and offer no door; the chronicle shows the live deadline (`test/guide2_wayThere.test.js` (6), `tools/mutants/guide2.json` 24/24 dead, Ledger A THE JOURNAL SAYS WHERE). GUIDE3 SHIPPED, THE HERALD: a quest's news - new, a new entry, under a day left, completed or ended - as one notice per quest in the enhanced notice stack, the title and the newest entry's opening, read aloud by the stack; fed by the quest bridge's tick (one quiet look, only while it listens) and drawn on drawHud's one call, so every host at once; the `quest-herald` switch, on (`test/guide3_herald.test.js` (8), `tools/mutants/guide3.json` 45/45 dead, Ledger A A QUEST'S NEWS IS A NOTICE). GUIDE4 SHIPPED, THE TRACKER: the quest the player follows as a card at the HUD's right-upper edge - the one tracked from the journal (a Track toggle in both journal faces, kept per character in the per-mod save slot), else the one the journal last changed - with its opening, where it points ("(you are here)" underfoot) and the time left; the party list steps under it; the Quests tab opens on it (`test/guide4_tracker.test.js` (9), `tools/mutants/guide4.json` 49/49 dead, Ledger A THE QUEST YOU FOLLOW, ON THE HUD). GUIDE5 SHIPPED, THE MARKS: every active quest's place on the held map (a gold diamond above the place, the followed one filled, a card on hover) and the followed quest's place on the enhanced compass on the street - only a place the player's map already holds (DFU's own find-place gate); a place named but not on the map gets the card's "ask around" note; the classic maps and compass stay DFU's (`test/guide5_marks.test.js` (8), `tools/mutants/guide5.json` 34/34 dead, Ledger A WHERE A QUEST POINTS, MARKED). AUDITED before it merges (`01-Overview/Audit-Guide.md`, AUDIT GUIDE): the HUD counts down only a deadline the journal names (the pause tab's "Time remains" is Mac's call, DECISIONS 8). Next: the accessible journal and its shelf, guidance tiers, DFU's quest debugger.

- `01-Overview/Desktop-App.md` (DA10 THE LAUNCHER STAYS) - 2026-09-29, Mac: "So this is an actual launcher now? Like warframe?" - "Yes please". The launcher is the game's front door: every launch, it waits for PLAY - the patch notes as its news (NEW since the player last played, UPDATE on its way), the update in its status bar, the player's own doors (game files, saves, the update check, a reinstall) beside them. Play is held only while an update is decided or the game has no files.
- `01-Overview/Desktop-App.md` (DA8 THE LAUNCHER, DA9 FINDING THE GAME FILES, REL4 THE RELEASE WHOLE, REL5 NAMES THAT DO NOT MOVE) - 2026-09-29, Mac: "How can we drastically improve the install experience? Is there anyway to send a notification that a new update is available ... A launcher? I really want to make it AAA grade". The shell's first window is a launcher: it installs an update BEFORE play where the copy can (a Windows update measured at ~4.5 MB of a 208 MB installer - a separate launcher program would make nothing smaller), shows what changed, finds the player's Daggerfall files (Steam, GOG, DFU's settings), and hands over at the game's first paint; a later update is told in the game and offered from the File menu. The shell's ARENA2 test was one file while the game's is seven (A2-WHOLE, still open in the app) - fixed. Releases are published whole, once, from a draft, with the player's patch notes (every release had carried its generated notes two or three times and gone live before its Windows files); the downloads lost their build number, so the site links each installer directly.

REST-OPT (Tabitha: "Allow party members to choose not to rest with their party"): a "Rest with my party" switch - off, the member rests on their own and the party rests without them. SHARE-MEND (Tabitha: "Fix sharing Guild & Temple quests"): a dungeon quest's markers travel slim so the share fits, a refusal between two builds says to reload, a restore failure is its own reason, and a sync's refusal is said once. TRADE-INFO (Tabitha: "Enhanced+ doesn't show enchants"): the Enhanced card and the trade window list a DFU magic item's powers. TRADE-FIT: an offer too big for one trade frame is refused in words instead of timing the trade out. CHAT-POST (Tabitha: "Link in chat / Post in chat"): the pack's card posts an item to the chat's open tab as one line - its name, its stat and its magic. HOME-STATIONS (Tabitha: "CRAFTABLE / PURCHASABLE ... GUILD STATIONS FOR HOMES / SHIPS"): a placed piece in a home, house or ship may be made an Alchemy, Spellmaking or Enchanting station for a one-time licence, and opens that craft's maker.

- `07-Rendering/Retro-Mode.md` - AUDIT RETRO1 2026-09-24, Mac ("Audit this"): FIVE LENSES OVER RETRO MODE - fidelity to DFU, WebGL2, the hosts, the pins and the docs, cost - AND EVERY CONFIRMED FINDING FIXED AND PINNED OR RECORDED. HIGH: Shift-F11 under any open window quick-loaded with no prompt - the two arms that let QuickLoad through a window read F11's code alone, so the settings screen's own toggle lost the player everything since the last quicksave (`retroToggleKey` guards both). MED: the docked twin came from the settings while the rect came from the drawn bar, and the enhanced skin draws none - a 320x154 image stretched over the whole canvas; now the texture, the lens and the rect all read the drawn bar. The effects ran on display bytes where DFU runs them between UnityCG's approximate gamma pair, so posterize's first level showed 17 where DFU's screen shows 13; the present now decodes, runs DFU's arithmetic and encodes. The image and its depth stayed bound on units 0 and 1 into the next frame that draws into them - a feedback loop for any sampler reading those units (the smooth grass read unit 0). The Morrowind arms were drawn into the retro image (on the classic set they are shown after it now). The 8 MB LUT froze a frame for ~200 ms (~750 at shift 0) and was never freed: it is built 4 ms a frame with the image shown plain meanwhile, and freed with retro off. Casual is the same world with five rules on what it costs, written out as data in `survival/difficulty.js`: stamina is the only price of neglect; a need charges only at the stages the HUD already paints red, at Hard's rates; never below half the pool, so the needs alone can never raise DFU's lethal exhaustion collapse; no gate, no second encounter ask, no stiff morning, no sickness roll, no hunt harm (each has a declared safe twin, on the same rolls), no blackout (the barkeep stops pouring before the coin changes hands). Every charging law takes its tier's rules and runs Hard when handed none; the compositions read the live tier where they already read the switch; the resting encounter roll takes a count of asks. A stored Off stays Off (SUPERSEDED IN PART by the audit above: Off is stored as the old switch's own `false`, never converted), everyone else moves to Casual - and every tier, Off included, stays the player's online. - **STATUS-LIVE + NOTICE-FIT** 2026-09-22, kurkku through Mac, in one message: "minor thing: would be nice if the info panel that comes up when you press i didn't pause the game, that way you could quickly check your status while walking around" and "I think we also need the sizing of the boxes to properly adjust for the text instead of always being wide". TWO DEFECTS WITH ONE CAUSE BETWEEN THEM, and the cause is a move that was right: ENH-NOTICE1 took DFU's centre-of-screen parchment and made it a panel at the right EDGE of the screen, and carried across two properties of the parchment that do not belong to a panel at the edge. It stopped the world, and it was a fixed slab as wide as the longest thing it would ever have to say. STATUS-LIVE: the pause was a leftover of the PRESENTATION, not a law of the thing shown. DFU pauses for the Status box because DisplayStatusInfo (DaggerfallUI.cs:1615-1628) is a DaggerfallMessageBox, a window raises `PauseWhileOpen` (UserInterfaceWindow.cs:141) and the parchment is in the middle of the screen with nothing visible past it - all true, and none of it true of a panel you can see the world behind. A READOUT IS THE PLAYER'S (AUDIT-WH R8, the law the hover plaque was built on), so the box declares DFU's own field `pauseWhileOpen: false` - which `ui/windowStack.js` has read off every window in every host's slot since ROAD-B - and all four pause latches answer false for it without a single new gate. Three things follow from not pausing. NO CHAIN: AddNextMessageBox advances on a dismissal and nothing routes this box a key, so the record-22 status text, the health box and SURV5's survival advice are ONE page, blank-line separated - which is what "quickly check your status" wanted anyway. NO ClickAnywhereToClose: a click while the world runs is a swing, so the box declines it and the panel's caption names the LIVE Status binding instead of promising a gesture it does not honour. AND IT YIELDS: the Status key is a toggle, Escape closes it and does nothing else, and any action that RAISES A WINDOW takes the slot the readout is standing in first - without which the dungeon's own free-slot guards would simply have refused the character sheet, which is a key that silently does nothing, the drawn-door-that-opens-nothing defect this port has paid for four times. The host gates that consumed every key and every click on the SLOT'S TRUTHINESS now ask the PAUSE, which is the shape `dungeonContext.js`'s twin has carried since ROAD-tail and the reason `interiorPaused` was written; the dungeon frame paints a non-pausing occupant on the PLAYED frame, because its paused arm returns above the draw; and the 60% modal DIM is gated on the window being modal, since a corridor the player is walking down cannot be blacked out. ONE COMPOSER, NOT FOUR: `world.js`, `exterior.js`, worldModes' interior arm and `dungeonContext.js` each wrote the same three-line chain out by hand, which is four places to forget when the law moves - it moved today, into `ui/statusBox.js`, with its live half in `systems/statusReadout.js` (a LEAF: `ui/input.js` owns the one door every window key comes through and is already inside `ui/actionText.js`'s import ring, and reaching for the box from there put its class body in a temporal dead zone - a real crash on a real import order, found by running it). NOTICE-FIT is the same panel's width: a fixed `width: min(520px, 70vw)` became a `max-width`, so a flex item under `align-items: flex-end` is shrink-to-fit and the rows set the width, wrapping only at the cap. Measured in Chromium by `tools/noticeFitProbe.mjs`: 210 / 450 / 520 / 339 px at 1280 wide, four widths where there used to be one. THE PROBE LIED ON ITS FIRST RUN and the picture is why it was caught - it photographed panels 46px off the right edge with a live transform, because NOTICE_WATCHDOG_MS had correctly swept a panel nobody was drawing; it pumps the draw the way a host does now. test/statuslive.test.js (10), tools/mutants/statuslive1.json (33/33 dead - three survived the first draft and all three were real: two because the caption pin pushed the binding store down itself instead of driving `bindings()` and `setBindings`, and one because a `&& !_live.done` term was dead code, since every door that raises `done` forgets the box in the same statement).
- **WATER-BACK + TRAVEL-FARE + ARREST-SHIELD** 2026-09-22, three Discord reports in one pass, and two of them are the port disagreeing with itself. WATER-BACK (kurkku, "invisible water"): THE BAND-AID OUTLIVED ITS BUG BY ONE DAY. AIWATER (2026-09-20) took the water out of every SPAWNED dungeon because "a spawn's water has been reported wrong every time - shown well below the floor, in patches, reading like a no-clip glitch", and said so honestly - "rather than keep chasing the placement". WATER-D1 (2026-09-21, the next morning) chased it and CAUGHT it, and it was never the placement: both dungeon hosts called `renderer.drawWater` AFTER drawFoes returned, which is after the first screen quad, which is where the enhanced-lighting lane resolves its frame target - so the quad landed on the default framebuffer, whose depth buffer holds no world, and passed the depth test everywhere. WATER-D1's own words: "The level itself was never the defect." A spawn was never special; it was where people met the bug because it was where people were. AND THE BAND-AID ONLY COVERED TWO OF THREE DOORS: the quad build and `blockWaterLevelAt` had the skip, `waterSurfaceYAt` - the swim TOGGLE and the drowning tick - never did, so a spawned dungeon had water that could still pull a player under and drown them with no plane drawn and no fog to say why. Invisible water in the dangerous sense. LostMyLeg's caution on the report ("when water textures are activated again they clip through walls") is a memory of the pre-WATER-D1 defect and is the right thing to be careful about, so the DRAW ORDER that makes it safe is now pinned where the re-enable lives. TRAVEL-FARE (kurkku: "really long trips don't cost anything when player-controlled cautious travel is disabled ... ship travel has the cost it should"): OL2 reasoned that online there are no nights, so no night is paid - right about the nights, WRONG ABOUT THE UNIT. DFU bills the trip's HOURS, and the journey has a length online even though the clock will not advance over it; OL2 already drew that line correctly one clause later for the ship ("a crossing is a crossing"). In practice: turn Travel Options' cautious travel off, the trip becomes an instant arrival, and online it cost no time AND no gold - free teleportation anywhere in the Bay from a mod toggle, while a ship still billed, which is exactly why it read as a bug rather than a rule. THE PIN I WROTE A DAY EARLIER MEASURED THE BUG AND CALLED IT THE LAW (travelinn: "and no night is paid"), which is the lesson: a pin that faithfully encodes a design will defend it after the design is wrong. The days stay zero - that half of OL2 is untouched and it is the half players asked for. ARREST-SHIELD (Revverie: you can die during a trial online, and the game freezes): the guard arm was right about the situation and too narrow about the attacker - online the court sequence cannot pause the world, so the boxes are read standing in the street, and a town's foes hunt every player in the cell. A bandit is not a guard; neither is a spell, a fall or drowning. Dying inside the court sequence puts a death screen and a modal trial on one window, each waiting for the other, which is the freeze. The one player damage door takes a VETO, in front of the shield pool and the SetHealth(0) branch both, and the arrest flow owns the single predicate the guard arm now reads too. 4+3+3 pins, 31 mutants, 30 dead, 1 equivalent; six older pins re-aimed and five stale mutant records re-aimed.
- **SWING-SAY** 2026-09-22, Mac ("its not working on the install but works on the browser. Each time I bring this up you avoid it"): THREE REPORTS, TWO SHIPPED FIXES, AND THE REPORTS CONTINUE - so this slice ships a READOUT rather than a third guess. MAC-SWING1 found a swing bound to a key that nothing read; MAC-D1 found an action bound to NOTHING and repaired it on load. Both are in every desktop release from app-v0.1.3638 on, checked by ancestry rather than by memory. The chain reads sound end to end from the source - swingButton/swingHeld for a mouse row, swingKeyHeld polled by all four hosts for a key row, the repair at the one load door - and the gesture FIRES in a driven test at ~10 pixels of travel, in the drag's own direction. So what is left is STATE, and the state is the one asymmetry there is: the browser keeps its store in localStorage and the desktop app keeps its own FILE (app/lib/fileStorage.cjs), which survives updates and reinstalls. One player, one machine, TWO STORES - and the app's is the older one. That is the exact shape of "works in the browser, not in the install", and no amount of reading src/ can see it. WHAT CANNOT BE READ CAN STILL BE MADE TO SPEAK: one line at the moment the binding store first becomes real, naming every code SwingWeapon answers to across both dicts, the DOM button it resolves to (-1 = the drag has no button to hold), whether it is reachable without a gamepad (the pad rows are refilled on every load, so an unbound swing still LOOKS bound), the swing MODE, and the travel a swing needs in pixels. It WARNS when the drag cannot work and logs when it can, so a player reading a console for the first time can tell the two apart without knowing what any field means. THE MODE IS THE LIKELIEST ANSWER AND THE READOUT WILL SETTLE IT: MAC-D1's own note records that both earlier reporters moved to CLICK to get an attack at all, that setting lives in the app's file, and in Click a held drag fires once on the press and never again - which is "the swiping does not work" with attacks still happening. TWO MUTANTS SURVIVED THE FIRST DRAFT and both were holes in the pins rather than in the code: the pad-row case passed even when the readout ignored the secondary dict (empty list, unreachable - right by accident), and nothing drove the boot line at all. IT REPAIRS NOTHING, and a pin holds that: a fix guessed from here would be a fix aimed at a machine nobody can see. 6 pins, 7 mutants, 7 dead.
- **FREEMOUSE** 2026-09-22, Mac ("Lets also add an entirely new keybind. A mouse free that allows you to toggle the use of your mouse"): THE TOGGLE ITSELF WAS NOT MISSING, AND THAT IS THE FINDING. DFU has `Actions.ActivateCursor`, PlayerMouseLook.cs:190-198 reads it, and the port has read it since U45 - what did not exist is a key that is ONLY the mouse. ActivateCursor's default is ENTER, and online Enter is also the chat's open (chatPanel.js CHAT_OPEN_ACTION), so the one press a player reaches for to free the mouse is the same press that opens a text box. PL3 had to WORK AROUND that rather than resolve it, because DFU's binding is DFU's and the port does not get to re-key it; a second ACTION resolves it instead of arguing with it. Enter keeps DFU's meaning, `FreeMouse` is the player's own, and the two are ORed at the ONE reader (`bindCursorToggle`) so there is no second toggle to keep in step - a second binding over `_cursorActive` is exactly the bug PL3 spent a slice on, and a host-side `e.code === 'KeyY'` is AUDIT 58's (a key-literal in a host makes a rebindable row inert in both directions). THE DEFAULT IS Y AND IT IS WHAT IS LEFT RATHER THAN WHAT IS APT: the pin RUNS the elimination rather than quoting it - DFU's table spends nineteen letters, the port spends F, J and P, and the vendored mods' defaults and OFFERED CHOICES spend B, G, K, O and X, which leaves exactly one. The obvious keys are all spoken for and each for a reason worth not undoing: Alt is Sneak, Backquote the console, Tab the pixel dial, Enter the very collision this exists to escape. It is an ACTION, so a player who wants it under their thumb moves it in the pane. THE OLD SUITE NOTICED FIRST, twice: a fixture that needed "a code no default uses" had been moved to KeyY by QUICK-LOOT B4 for exactly that reason, and FreeMouse taking Y broke it - that line is a CANARY for the keymap being full, not an ordinary fixture, and it is recorded as one now (it moved to F7, which DFU's table skips). The row is APPENDED, YIELDS in the classic windows, and is drawn in the enhanced pane under its own 'Mouse' heading rather than under 'Online': the collision is an online one, but freeing the mouse is something you do to read the screen. 5 pins, 11 mutants, 11 dead; six older enumerating pins widened and one drifted Ledger citation re-resolved.
- **QUICK-LOOT-STATS** 2026-09-22, a player relayed by Mac ("i love the quick loot but can it show the stats of the items next to the quickloot window? So i dont have to pick up everything to check in my inventory if its worth keeping"): THE ARC WAS ONLY HALF MOVED. Arc A said what a pile HELD and Arc B let you take it, so "is this worth stopping for" could be answered under the crosshair while "is this better than what I am wearing" still cost a pickup, a menu and a drop - the decision the whole arc exists to move into the world was still going through a door. A player found the gap in a day. ONE ROW, NOT SIX: the panel describes the row the player has PICKED OUT, because that is the one they are deciding about and because six stat blocks under a crosshair is a window, which is the thing this arc exists to avoid - so it needs no switch of its own, it is the highlight's other half. `itemInfo.js itemStatRows` is a THIRD PRESENTER over the producers MAC-M1 already shared between the classic popup's macro pass and the enhanced detail card, so a longsword cannot hit for 1-15 under the crosshair and 3-17 in the pack. IT SHOWS WHAT THE INVENTORY SHOWS AND NOT ONE ROW MORE, and the deliberate omission is the VALUE: `itemValueOf` is one read away and "worth keeping" sounds like it is asking for one, but the card carries no value, so a value row would not be sparing the player a trip to the pack - it would be inventing a number that surface never had, and a misleading one, because what a shop pays runs through `calculateCost` with the shop's quality and the haggling in it. THE SIDE IS ARITHMETIC AND THE NUMBERS LIVE WITH THE DRESS: `statsSide` picks right, then left, then BELOW (the phone's honest answer, in the plaque's own flow rather than hanging off an edge), and the widths are imported from enhancedStyle.js rather than restated, because a layout decided against a number the CSS no longer uses is a panel half off the screen. FOUR MUTANTS SURVIVED THE FIRST DRAFT AND EVERY ONE WAS A HOLE: the two CONDITIONAL rows were only ever exercised on a weapon and a cuirass, where neither asks its question; the row guard was tested against a DOOR, which has no rows either way, rather than against a highlight belonging to another pile; and the sheet/arithmetic pin compared the CSS against the very constant the CSS is built from, so it agreed by construction - a pin asserting the wiring as it is always agrees with it. A fifth found that nothing drove the repaint guard's new term, so a resize under a still crosshair would have left the panel hanging off the edge it was laid out against; it is driven now, in worldhover.test.js beside the DOM harness. AND THEN IT WAS PHOTOGRAPHED, because "next to" is a thing about pixels: `tools/quickLootStatsProbe.mjs` draws the plaque and its panel through the real `showWorldPlaque` over the real sheet in Chromium, at three widths, walking the highlight down a pile of four. It found two faults on its first run that no pin in the suite could have failed on - a wrapped Condition value whose second line sat against the LABEL and read as a different row, and the survival SENTENCE rows inheriting that fix's right alignment and reading as a value with no name. The rows were right, the side was right, the markup was right, and the box looked wrong. 8 pins, 15 mutants, 14 dead, 1 recorded equivalent, 1 withdrawn as not naming a law; three older row-shape pins re-aimed and three stale mutant records re-aimed.
- **MENU-RELOCK** 2026-09-22, Fedu on Discord (a 1-3 second delay before the camera answers the mouse again after closing a menu), fix drafted in PR #316 and taken over here: POINTER LOCK IS ONLY GRANTED INSIDE A USER GESTURE. The browser hands `requestPointerLock` out on the keypress the player just made and refuses it anywhere else - silently, with nothing retrying - so the look stays dead until the player's NEXT gesture happens to reach a relock arm. The delay is not a timer; it is however long it takes you to press something else, which is exactly why three people reported three different durations. THE PORT ASKED A FRAME LATE: `makeLookGate` is the per-host reconciler and it runs on the FRAME, seeing the overlay slot drain and relocking on the next call - a new task, no user activation left. The fix is not a faster gate but the four hosts asking inside the key handler that closed the window, on BOTH edges, since a two-phase window like the automap closes on key-UP and that release is a gesture too. THE INCOMING PATCH WAS RIGHT ABOUT THE CAUSE AND BROKE TWO PINS ON THE WAY: it put `const hadOverlay` between `if (mode === 'interior') {` and its routeKey call, and U43's one-dispatch law is that the interior arm routes the whole table over its own ctx with NOTHING standing in between (qs7_one_dispatch, flagsweep). The read is hoisted above the mode ladder now, where one read serves both arms because only one ctx is live per mode. Its four tests were source greps that would have passed if the fix were pasted into a comment; they are replaced by pins that DRIVE the gate and measure the gap - nothing has asked at the moment of the key, one request lands a frame later - and check the call sites by SHAPE rather than by string. ONE MUTANT FOUND A HOLE IN THE NEW PIN rather than in the source: `relock` appears twice in world.js, so a whole-file match let the host seam be renamed and still passed, while `host.relock?.()` is optional-chained and a missing seam is SILENT - every relock would simply never happen. It reads the literal's slice now. 3 pins, 10 mutants, 10 dead.
- **SHIP-PORTS** 2026-09-23, Sir McMobdon on Discord ("cant access my boat anymore after the travel update", then, having turned Travel Options' port rule off, "Still dont work ... Nether has options for the boat"): A RESTRICTION NOBODY CHOSE, WITH NO SWITCH TO REACH. Roleplay & Realism's `shipPorts` module (on in the mod, so on under the vendored-defaults rule) restricts the T picker's Ship row to a port town - Hazelnut's port list with Travel Options on - and the merge switched it on for every boat owner at once. The Features home curates three of the mod's switches and this was not one, so the player could not turn it off short of the whole mod. Two fixes: `shipPorts` is curated on the home, and it ships OFF - the one recorded departure from the mod's defaults (the same reasoning as Climates & Calories staying optional: a rule the player never asked for should not take something away silently). The defaults pin names it as the exception. Scourge Barrow's "a door wont open" (same day, #general) was not the door's law at all: it is DISC19-E (`01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-24.md`) - the throne-room door stands inside the corridor piece's placement box, and the press and the swing picked that box's walk-on record instead.
- **DISC14** 2026-09-23. A, Starempire42 on Discord ("is there a way to make it so I can see my weapon above my horse?"): the weapon rig drew before the mount, and DFU's horse is at GUI.depth 2, behind the weapons - both riding hosts draw the rig after the mount now. B, Mac with a screenshot of Weapon Widget's tile ("these need to be the default values ingame for diverse weapons. The current defaults are wrong on the screen"): Diverse Weapons' preset ships OFF again, and Weapon Widget ships Mac's values - its own defaults with DoubleScaleTextures on and Inertia.Scale 0, two recorded departures from the mod's defaults; the tile now shows what draws. `Field-Bugs-2026-09-23.md` DISC14. B was reverted the next day by DISC16-B.

Every populated region's main city is its hub (`systems/regionHubs.js`: the best kind of settlement, then the one named for the region, then the largest; the game's own rows alone, so every client agrees; Daggerfall, Wayrest and Sentinel are capitals). Online the held map puts a hub in a blue circle, a capital in purple, names it on the label and in the I box, and walking in says so. 6 pins, 30 mutants. `06-Systems/Online-Arc.md` THE HOLDINGS ARC, HUB1.

Online the bank sells no house; offline nothing changes. `06-Systems/Online-Arc.md` HOME1.

- **DW-CLIP** 2026-09-23, two Discord reports on Diverse Weapons relayed by Mac ("mod should be defaulted on and theres an issue with the sprites getting cut off"): THE PRESET'S IDLES WERE HALF OFF THE SCREEN. The clone's DoubleScaleTextures arm adds half the rect to the Offset channel on an idle so the doubled `w_` repaint, drawn into a box UpdateWeapon made twice the classic frame, lands where the classic one stood - and the port applied that shift whenever the module was on. The mod's preset turns DoubleScaleTextures AND TrueTextureSize on, and under TrueTextureSize the box is the painting's own size, not doubled: the mod's `w_` idles are full-canvas paintings (the war axe's 317x200 IS the 320x200 screen), so half a screen's shift right and down left a snippet. The shift rides the doubling now - the module without TrueTextureSize, and a `w_` hit, the one case the box is doubled; a plain hit and the classic frame were never doubled and are not shifted either. And the preset defaults ON (it was off at DW1 because a preset nobody selected is not selected; the first player to meet the mod had to go looking for the switch). Every widget bench says the preset is off explicitly (MO1's rule), since Weapon Widget's own numbers are their subject. 1 pin over the war axe whole and the three offsets, 2 mutants (38 over dw1, all dead), four pins re-aimed.
- **MODS-ONLINE-5** 2026-09-23, Mac (asked which of Roleplay & Realism's switches to force online "especially when it comes to balance", then "Yeah its the reason we forced PCAAO"): ONE RULESET PER ROOM, SAID ONCE AND APPLIED TO BOTH. MODS-ONLINE-4 forced PCAAO whole, and the reason it gave was the host's foes - but PCAAO also rewrites the player's OWN blows, and that half was forced too without saying why. RR's six combat overrides (advanced archery, weapon speed, weapon materials, the classic strength bonus, equipment damage, encumbrance) are the same kind of thing - FormulaHelper overrides on the striker's own blow, the wearer's own armor, the walker's own load - so under the precedent's real reason they take the same answer, forced to the mod's own shipped defaults (the classic bonus ships off and stays off). And one that is an exploit rather than a preference: intensive training spends four days of world time for its +4, and the shared clock refuses the days (CLOCK-REFUSAL - the prison release's lesson), so online it would be four skill points for nothing; forced OFF, which is what the mod ships. Everything of RR's that reaches nobody stays the player's - bandaging, climbing, loans, ship ports, beds, the douse, the riding movement and trample, variable training prices, the expulsion (its squad is the expelled player's own consequence). Twenty forced switches in the shelf. The menu's lock copy gained a THIRD reason (the ruleset's), because the shared note's words - "a dungeon's foes are its host's and loot changes hands" - are not why a weapon-speed rule is locked, and a lock that gives the wrong reason is a refusal nobody can read. 1 pin, 2 mutants (18 over modsonline1, all dead).
- **MODS-ONLINE-4** 2026-09-22, Mac ("So how does this all work online?" then "What about player balance?"): THREE MODS BACK TO THE ROOM, AND THE LESSON IS THE QUESTION. Three passes had asked "does it desync?" and never asked "does my switch reach another player?". The port IS owner-authoritative, so nothing freed had broken a room - but OWNERSHIP IS NOT PRIVACY, and two of the places a machine owns something are places its answer is handed to everybody else. A world room's LAYOUT FOES ARE ITS HOST'S (WORLD2), so the host's Meaner Monsters and PCAAO are not "the host's own game" - they are what the whole party fights, and a host with either off runs a softer dungeon for four people who never chose that and cannot see why. ITEMS CHANGE HANDS: a corpse's pile is granted to peers (WORLD6b-iii(c)) and a room's containers are shared (WORLD4), so Unleveled Loot's rolls do not stay with the roller, and one player rolling level-scaled drops and passing them across is two rulesets feeding one economy. Those three are the room's now - not because a room where they differ falls apart, but because a room where they differ is one player's setting spending someone else's evening. CLIMATES & CALORIES WAS RE-ASKED THE SAME QUESTION AND KEPT THE PLAYER'S, deliberately: it reaches another player only through a corpse's FOOD - meat and rations, no power and no gear - and forcing it means nobody may opt out of freezing to death, which is a live complaint (DragynDance). So are the Shield Widget, Handheld Torches, Oblivion leveling and Travel Options, which only ever make the player's own run harder or easier. Five switches forced in the whole shelf now: the two that decide the GROUND and the three that spend a stranger's evening. The menu's lock copy is SPLIT - the three are not locked for the ground's reason, and a lock that gives the wrong reason is as useless as no reason at all. 3 mutants added (16 over modsonline1, all dead), pins re-aimed in modsonline and onlinelane.
- **MODS-ONLINE-3** 2026-09-22, Mac ("What part of mod toggle for online do you not understand?"): THE TWO MOD ROWS THAT WERE NOT IN THE MOD STORE. MODS-ONLINE-2 freed the sixteen VENDORED mods and stopped, because Climates & Calories and Weapon Sheathing are carried on the PREFS shelf - the port wrote both systems itself, from Ralzar's and Greatness7's work - and the sweep walked MOD_SETTINGS. That distinction exists nowhere a player can see it: both sit in the Mods pane under their authors' names, beside sixteen rows that were all free, and both were still locked. THE ANSWER WAS NOT "it is not really a mod", it was to sweep by what a player SEES - every feature row whose kinds carry `mod`, whichever store holds its switch. Both pass the same reading the sixteen did. The survival system writes only the entity handed to it: hunger, thirst, exposure and the felt temperature are minted fresh each tick from MY climate, MY clothes and MY race onto MY entity, a camp is local (the braziers are scenery the terrain carries either way), and a shop's stock is its own - the one thing that leaves the machine is a corpse's food, minted by the KILLER, and a corpse's pile is ALREADY the owner's word granted to peers (WORLD6b-iii(c)), which is Unleveled Loot's exact shape and Unleveled Loot is the player's. Weapon Sheathing was forced "so every body a peer sees wears its blade the same way" - a claim about how MY machine DRAWS someone else, which is `peerClassSprites`'s category and has always been the player's; a scabbard stands no object, rolls nothing and never reaches the wire, and the pose's `wd` carries a peer's drawn weapon either way. THE ENTITY CLAIM IS MEASURED, not argued: the new pin runs ten hours of a snowbound night on one body and reads the other back byte-identical. One row stays forced and is NAMED rather than left to a silent list - `enhancedEnvironments`, the port's own enhanced outdoors, which carries the shared weather's evolution and wears a mod kind only because Dynamic Skies opens its knobs on that tile (Dynamic Skies itself is the player's). ONE MUTANT WAS WITHDRAWN rather than recorded equivalent: it mutated the pin's OWN assertion, and a test cannot fail a mutation of itself. 2 pins, 2 mutants added (13 over modsonline1, 36 over ws1), all dead, two older source pins re-aimed and one stale mutant record re-aimed.
- **MODS-ONLINE-2** 2026-09-22, Mac ("Is it possible to allow all mods to be toggled on and off for online?" and then, of the first answer, "So all mods can now be toggled?"): NOW THEY CAN - fifteen of sixteen entirely, and the sixteenth in every switch but the two that move the GROUND. MODS-ONLINE freed eight and left eight forced because they sounded like world state; this half is a reading of the PORT instead of the names, and it overturns one of that pass's own rulings. THE PORT IS ALREADY OWNER-AUTHORITATIVE EVERYWHERE A MOD COULD DISAGREE: a blow's damage is the STRIKER's number and the host applies it without recomputing (applyHit: "The number is a peer's word and the host trusts it"), so PCAAO's formulas were never shared; a foe's stats are minted where it SPAWNS and a peer steps a puppet, so a joiner already fights the host's foes under the host's numbers; a corpse's pile is rolled and granted by the owner's word (WORLD6b-iii(c)); and a blow is mitigated where it LANDS - damageShieldPool inside damageFoe for a foe and inside hurtPlayer for me - so the SHIELD WIDGET's block is the defender's own, which is the opposite of what MODS-ONLINE concluded from its name. Oblivion leveling is written into a character at creation, Handheld Torches is an item in my save with a light on my screen, Travel Options is my own journey. None of it crosses as a RULE; it crosses as a RESULT, which is what the wire carries. ONE THING IS NOT A RULE - IT IS THE FLOOR: Basic Roads rewrites TERRAIN HEIGHTS (terrainGen calls smoothRoadHeights over the beds), so `Enabled` and `SmoothRoads` decide where the ground IS, and a pose is a position on that ground. AND THE OLD LANE HAD THAT BACKWARDS, which is the finding worth keeping: it forced `Enabled` and left `SmoothRoads` to the player because `SmoothRoads` is a DIAL, so the heights have been diverging online since the lane was written for anyone who turned the smoothing off for the "minor extra performance" its own description offers - forcing the mod bought the room nothing while its own dial gave the floor away. The table is by KEY now. `RiversAndStreams` is deliberately out of it and that is MEASURED, not argued: the pin runs the real smoother over every tile the river and stream tables can write and reads the heights back unchanged, because SMOOTHED_TILES is the road bed and the painter lays a road before it considers water. The player-facing copy was carrying two claims that had stopped being true and both are rewritten, with the two locked road rows given their OWN reason rather than the lane's. THE SURVIVING MUTANT FOUND A SECOND HOLE rather than a missing assertion: modSettings treated anything truthy at `keys[key]` as a declaration, so every name on Object.prototype was one - `toString` read past "is not a declared switch" and a write of it coerced against a function and stored it; the same mistake in the lane would have handed `room['toString']` back as a forced setting value. All three doors take own keys now. 7 pins, 12 mutants, 12 dead, two older pin files re-aimed.
- **DEATH-BODY1** 2026-09-22, Muriel on Discord ("Using the linux appimage, if you die in the tutorial dungeon and press F11 to load, you load the game but are visually a corpse. You can act normally, but are a corpse"), kurkku ("oh yeah I've noticed the same thing... must be some bug with Eye of the Beholder") and trashBattery, who confirmed it on Arch and NAMED THE CAUSE: "zooming into first person and then back out into 3rd person fixes it. looks like loading the game after a death doesn't update the character model state." He was exactly right. `died` in player/eotbBody.js is a LATCH - raised from the live signal, it then blocks every update below and holds the frozen death clip - and the ONLY thing that lowers it is `initialize`, whose only caller is `toggle` going active, which is precisely what scrolling out of first person and back in does. AND THE PIN THAT COVERED IT DEFENDED THE BUG: eotb_audit2's death pin ticked `died: false` twelve times and asserted the table was STILL Idle, citing the IL. The pin was faithful and the MOD IS RIGHT - in Daggerfall Unity a death ends the run, so the only way back is a load, and a load rebuilds the scene and the billboard object with it; the mod CANNOT have this bug. The port revives a module-level body instead - a quickload straight back into play, the online respawn, a prison release - and not one of those is an Initialize, so the latch outlived the death that set it. THE FIX IS AT THE SIGNAL, NOT AT ANY ONE CALLER: the body follows the ENTITY, so coming back to life lowers the latch wherever the life came from and every revival route is covered at once - a load hook would have left the respawn and the prison release still frozen, and the pin refuses that shape by name. A deliberate, documented divergence from EOTB-IL inside an otherwise IL-verbatim file, which is the lesson worth keeping: a pin that faithfully encodes the reference will defend a bug the reference could never have had. 1 pin, 3 mutants, 3 dead, one older pin re-aimed.

OL1's law is that a room plays one game: what one player walks through, another walks through. AUDIT-WH R8 had already drawn the line inside it for World Tooltips - that reasoning is about the WORLD, so a mod that moves a light, stands an object, changes a roll or writes a save record is what the room has to agree on, and one that draws a readout is not - and only `Enabled` was ever forced anyway; a mod's own dials were always the player's. Seven mods join it: dynamic-skies (the sky over a weather WORLD5 already shares - this only paints it), seasons-iliac-bay (which texture archives load), weapon-widget (its motion carries no damage, timing or attack state), eye-of-the-beholder (third person and your own billboard; peers are drawn by remotePlayers either way), ambient-text, immersive-footsteps and better-ambience. Eight stay the room's. TWO CANDIDATES FAILED THE READING, which is the whole reason it has to be one: the SHIELD WIDGET sounds cosmetic and carries `hitShield(damage, item)` and a block coroutine - it is in the damage path, and two players disagreeing about whether a blow was blocked is the room disagreeing about the blow; HANDHELD TORCHES sounds like a light and is an equipped ITEM in the save with a light standing in the world. The classification is TOTAL and pinned - every vendored mod on one side with its reason, never both, never neither, so a mod added tomorrow fails until somebody decides - and the reading itself is held by execution: each player's-side module is swept for the wire, a save record or a roll. One pin's own subject short-circuited at first (the dial test took the first mod with a dial, which is now the player's, and a player's mod returns before the key is looked at); it takes a room mod's dial now. 4 pins, 6 mutants, 6 dead.

So a ray cast straight down from a player standing in open country hit NOTHING, campSpot answered a null ground, and campDecision refused with "There is no level ground here" - on the flattest meadow in the Iliac Bay, everywhere outdoors, for every kit. THE DOOR THAT FIXES IT IS TWO DAYS OLD AND WAS BUILT FOR THE SAME MISTAKE: `surfaceHit`, from MAC-BUG W5, Mac's own "blood doesn't work outside" - the same collider, the same wrong reading, and its note names this caller without knowing it ("a caller that reads 'nothing' as 'no surface' is right indoors and silently wrong in the whole outdoors"). Blood was moved onto it and camping was not. It answers whichever is NEARER so a camp under a walkway still finds the walkway, and an older collider keeps the bucket probe rather than falling to null; a sweep of the other downward probes found no third victim. AND THE NOTIFICATION SPAM, from the other end: a toast system that stacks one line ten times is wrong whatever posted it. The classic column SCROLLS, so a repeat costs a row and passes; the enhanced skin draws a PLATE per row (ENH-NOTICE3), so ten copies of "You are freezing" are ten plates and nothing else can be read. An identical line already at the BACK of the queue now refreshes that row's dwell instead of adding a second, keeping its id so the plate is refreshed rather than flickering out and back - the back ONLY, because "You are hit" twice with something between them is two blows and both belong on screen. DEATHLOOP2 closed the loop that was producing them; this closes the ring that was willing to draw them. 6 pins, 7 mutants, 7 dead.

MAC-D3 fixed the ORDER of the revival and not its CONTENT - it moved the heal to the top so no frame could see a dead player with no death screen, which closed the window between reviving and landing; the health came back and the CAUSE did not go away, so a poisoned character woke at half health and the poison emptied it again in seconds, for ever. The loop is DRIVEN rather than read: a pin asserting "reviveForPlay is called" would have passed against the shipped bug too, because the shipped bug called a heal as well. Tried all the different reflex options, but I can only click the Back button"): A CHARACTER COULD NOT BE CREATED AT ALL, and the button was COVERED rather than dead. Both first guesses were wrong and are pinned as such - it is never disabled, and the flow advances fine (headless, confirm at reflexes reaches summary). `.choose` is centred at `height: 100%` and `.stagebody`'s implicit grid row is `auto`, which is max-content, so a stage taller than the pane kept its full height while `.stagebody` shrank under `flex:1; min-height:0` - and the difference spilled out of the bottom as VISIBLE overflow, under the opaque `.actionbar` laid out straight after it. `elementFromPoint` at the button's own centre answers `DIV.actionbar` at 1280x720. REFLEXES IS THE ONLY `.choose` STAGE WITH A CONTINUE (sex, class method and biography method all advance on the ANSWER) and the tallest at 748px, five answers at `min-height: 88px` - the one screen where this could strand anybody - AND IT IS GREEN AT 1920x1080, which is why it shipped. Measured rather than read, because node cannot see a painted rectangle: tools/chargenReflexProbe.mjs reads elementFromPoint at eight window sizes, five red before the fix, and SHOW_BUG=1 re-proves it - the FIRST probe could only print OK because it reverted the CSS and not the markup, and the one before that measured my own hand-written page instead of the wizard's real height chain. THE FIX IS TWO HALVES: the layout can give way (`minmax(0,1fr)` rows, `place-content: safe center` + `overflow-y: auto` - plain `center` puts half the overflow above the start edge where nothing can scroll to it), and the primary action leaves the scrolling stage - a stage DECLARES its confirm and the action bar draws it beside Back, always on screen. The three mandatory-path stages are converted (name, biography rep box, reflexes); four short card panes are named in a KNOWN list that may shrink and never grow, LEFT DELIBERATELY because this session has no ARENA2 and converting four screens that cannot be rendered risks a worse regression. 3 pins, 8 mutants, 8 dead.

By execution: all 265 quests parse, all 265 save/restore/save identical, the registry whole, all 188 offerable rows have files with the `-` marker honoured, the journal chain wired, and every one of the 28 `hooks.world.X` the engine reads either answered by world.js's questWorld or accounted for (3 talk* layered by talkMacroHooks, 4 court* superseded by arrestFlow's own value map). AN AUDIT THAT FINDS NOTHING IS WORTH NOTHING TOMORROW, so each link is a law now - and writing them is what found what the hand sweep had not. PIN 2 ASSERTED A STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE FAILURE: it looked for a `null` in `task.actions`, and `_readTaskLines` pushes only truthy actions and pends the raw text of anything else, so it could not have failed under any mutation. Reading `pendingActionLines` instead turned up FOUR LINES IN THE CORPUS THAT MATCH NO TEMPLATE - an unknown op-code the source itself flags, a dotted place symbol DFU's own PcAt reads as `\w+`, a `location` line the source says is not understood, and the demo's juggle, whose JuggleAction is COMMENTED OUT of DFU's registrations - each checked against Interkarma/daggerfall-unity @2343305d1 and pending there too, so none is a port gap and a FIFTH now fails the pin. PIN 3'S FIXTURE WAS TOO CLEAN TO CARRY THE FAULT: it round-tripped PRISTINE quests, where a constructor default and a saved default are the same value, so deleting a restore line changed nothing; quests are DIRTIED first now and the restoring clock is deliberately different. And a round trip has a blind spot dirtying cannot cover - a field the quest never WRITES round-trips perfectly and still loses the data - so the envelope is checked for what it CAPTURED too (the mutant that dropped `addLogStep`'s time stamp proved it: the journal dates every entry by it). THE REGISTRY LAW THAT WAS ACTUALLY MISSING is DFU's ORDER - `getActionTemplate` is a first-match scan, so a slot that moves changes which template claims a line, and it is 82 FOR 82 POSITIONALLY EXACT against DFU (eight documented renames, one declared WorldUpdate guard). auditquest_patterns.test.js compares those patterns far more deeply but SKIPS without a DFU checkout, which is CI's normal state, so the order was unguarded exactly where it matters; the fixture runs everywhere. RECORDED NOT ASSERTED: scenes/exterior.js's questWorld is 16 keys behind world.js's - the dev scene reached by `?region=&loc=` with no save path, so it is not held to the shipped surface, but promoting it cannot quietly inherit the gap. 5 pins, 13 mutants, 13 dead.
